     9→| Operation | Strategy | Why |
    10→|---|---|---|
    11→| load, hash, build | serde (`toml`) | Need typed `Spec` struct for build logic |
    12→| set, unset | `toml_edit` (`DocumentMut`) | Surgical edit preserving comments/formatting |
    13→| backup, restore, new -f | raw `fs::copy` | Exact byte-for-byte preservation |
    14→
    15→## Steps
    16→
    17→### Step 1: Add `toml_edit` dependency
    18→
    19→**File:** `Cargo.toml`
    20→
    21→Add `toml_edit = "0.22"` to `[dependencies]`. Already a transitive dep via `toml 0.8`, so no new download.
    22→
    23→### Step 2: Create `src/ts_cmd/edit.rs` - toml_edit helper module
    24→
    25→New module with:
    26→
    27→- **`FIELD_REGISTRY`** - static list of all valid field paths with their types:
    28→  ```
    29→  ("panic", Scalar)           ("strip", Scalar)
    30→  ("cargo.profile", Scalar)   ("cargo.target_triple", Scalar)
    31→  ("cargo.target_json", Scalar) ("cargo.target_dir", Scalar)
    32→  ("cargo.unstable", Array)   ("rustc.opt_level", Scalar)
    33→  ("rustc.panic", Scalar)     ("rustc.lto", Scalar)
    34→  ("rustc.codegen_units", Scalar) ("rustc.build_std", Array)
    35→  ("rustc.flags", Array)      ("linker.args", Array)
    36→  ```
    37→  (Skip `linker.version_script` for now - it's a nested table with `global` array + `local` string, more complex.)
    38→
    39→- **`validate_key(key) -> Result<FieldKind>`** - checks key against registry, returns `Scalar` or `Array`
    40→
    41→- **`set_field(doc, key, value) -> Result<()>`** - for scalars: parse key path → ensure table exists → set `doc["section"]["field"] = value`. Value parsing: booleans as `toml_edit::value(true/false)`, integers as `toml_edit::value(i64)`, everything else as string. For arrays: parse `[a,b,c]` or `"a","b"` syntax → replace the array.
    42→
    43→- **`unset_field(doc, key) -> Result<()>`** - parse key path → remove from table. If table becomes empty, remove the table too.
    44→
    45→- **`validate_value(key, value) -> Result<()>`** - reuse existing parse functions (`parse_panic_mode`, `parse_strip_mode`, etc.) for enum keys. Accept anything for string/array keys.
    46→
    47→### Step 3: Rewrite `src/ts_cmd/set.rs` to use toml_edit
    48→
    49→Replace serde round-trip with:
    50→1. Read file as raw string (or empty string if file doesn't exist)
    51→2. Parse into `DocumentMut`
    52→3. Validate key via `edit::validate_key()`
    53→4. Validate value via `edit::validate_value()` (for enum fields)
    54→5. Call `edit::set_field(doc, key, value)`
    55→6. Write `doc.to_string()` back to file
    56→
    57→Remove `apply_value()` and all parse helper functions from set.rs (move validation to edit.rs).
    58→
    59→Update tests to test the new toml_edit-based flow. Add tests for previously-missing keys (`rustc.build_std`, `linker.args`, `cargo.unstable`, etc.).
    60→
    61→### Step 4: Create `src/ts_cmd/unset.rs` - new command
    62→
    63→New file:
    64→1. Resolve package dir and tspec path (same pattern as set.rs)
    65→2. Read file as raw string
    66→3. Parse into `DocumentMut`
    67→4. Validate key via `edit::validate_key()`
    68→5. Call `edit::unset_field(doc, key)`
    69→6. Write back
    70→
    71→### Step 5: Wire up `ts unset` in CLI
    72→
    73→**Files:** `src/cmd/ts.rs`, `src/ts_cmd/mod.rs`
    74→
    75→- Add `Unset` variant to `TsCommands` enum (same args as `Set` minus the value):
    76→  ```
    77→  Unset { key, package, tspec }
    78→  ```
    79→- Add `unset_value` to `ts_cmd/mod.rs` re-exports
    80→- Add match arm in `TsCmd::execute()`
    81→
    82→### Step 6: Convert `ts backup` to raw file copy
    83→
    84→**Files:** `src/ts_cmd/backup.rs`, `src/tspec.rs`
    85→
    86→Current flow: `load_spec` → `save_spec_snapshot` (serde serialize) - destroys comments.
    87→
    88→New flow:
    89→1. Find the spec path (same as now)
    90→2. `load_spec` **only for hashing** (to compute the hash for the backup filename)
    91→3. Compute next sequence number (same logic as `save_spec_snapshot`)
    92→4. `fs::copy(source_path, backup_path)` - exact byte-for-byte copy
    93→
    94→Extract the sequence-number logic from `save_spec_snapshot` into a helper `next_snapshot_seq(name, dir)` in `tspec.rs` so both old code and new backup can use it.
    95→
    96→### Step 7: Convert `ts restore` to raw file copy
    97→
    98→**File:** `src/ts_cmd/restore.rs`
    99→
   100→Current flow: `load_spec(backup)` → `save_spec(target)` - destroys comments.
   101→
   102→New flow:
   103→1. Find backup path (same as now)
   104→2. Parse base name (same as now)
   105→3. `fs::copy(backup_path, target_path)` - exact copy
   106→
   107→### Step 8: Convert `ts new -f` to raw file copy
   108→
   109→**File:** `src/ts_cmd/new.rs`
   110→
   111→Current flow: `load_spec(source)` → `save_spec(target)` - destroys comments.
   112→
   113→New flow when `--from` is provided:
   114→1. Resolve source spec path (same as now)
   115→2. `fs::copy(source_path, output_path)` - exact copy
   116→
   117→When `--from` is NOT provided, keep using serde `save_spec(&Spec::default(), ...)` since there's no source to copy.
   118→
   119→### Step 9 (nice-to-have): Array append/remove syntax in `ts set`
   120→
   121→Extend `ts set` to support:
   122→- `tspec ts set linker.args+=-Wl,--gc-sections` (append to array)
   123→- `tspec ts set linker.args-=-Wl,--gc-sections` (remove from array)
   124→- `tspec ts set linker.args=["-static","-nostdlib"]` (replace entire array, from step 3)
   125→
   126→This builds on the `edit::set_field` infrastructure. Can be deferred if the basic set/unset is solid.
   127→
   128→### Step 10: Update todo.md
   129→
   130→Mark completed items, remove resolved entries.
   131→
   132→## Files to modify
   133→
   134→| File | Change |
   135→|---|---|
   136→| `Cargo.toml` | Add `toml_edit` dep |
   137→| `src/ts_cmd/edit.rs` | **NEW** - toml_edit helpers |
   138→| `src/ts_cmd/set.rs` | Rewrite to use toml_edit |
   139→| `src/ts_cmd/unset.rs` | **NEW** - unset command |
   140→| `src/ts_cmd/mod.rs` | Add unset + edit modules |
   141→| `src/cmd/ts.rs` | Add Unset variant |
   142→| `src/ts_cmd/backup.rs` | Use fs::copy |
   143→| `src/ts_cmd/restore.rs` | Use fs::copy |
   144→| `src/ts_cmd/new.rs` | Use fs::copy for --from |
   145→| `src/tspec.rs` | Extract `next_snapshot_seq()` helper |
   146→| `notes/todo.md` | Update status |
   147→
   148→## Verification
   149→
   150→```bash
   151→tspec test -p tspec          # All existing + new tests pass
   152→tspec clippy                 # No warnings
   153→tspec fmt --check            # Formatted
   154→
   155→# Manual smoke tests:
   156→tspec ts set rustc.build_std='["core","alloc"]'    # Previously: "unknown key"
   157→tspec ts set linker.args='["-static"]'             # Previously: "unknown key"
   158→tspec ts unset rustc.lto                           # New command
   159→tspec ts backup && diff original backup            # Byte-identical
   160→tspec ts restore -t name-001-hash                  # Preserves comments
   161→```
   162→

```
     1→# Chores-2
     2→
     3→Continued maintenance tasks, following on from chores-1.
     4→
     5→## 20260206 - Ts Execute Conversion
     6→
     7→### Convert `Commands::Ts` to use Execute trait
     8→
     9→The `Ts` variant in `Commands` is the only command that doesn't use the `Execute` trait. All other commands (Build, Run, Test, Clean, Clippy, Fmt, Compare, Version, Install) follow the pattern where a command struct implements `Execute` and main.rs calls `cmd.execute(&project_root)`. The `Ts` subcommands instead destructure fields in main.rs and call free functions from `ts_cmd::*`.
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/wink/data/prgs/rust/tspec/notes/chores-3.md",
  "content": "# Chores-3\n\nContinued maintenance tasks, following on from chores-2.\n\n## 20260209 - ts set/unset/backup/restore improvements using toml_edit\n\n### Context\n\n`ts set` currently only supports ~10 hardcoded scalar keys and silently ignores all array/nested fields (`rustc.build_std`, `rustc.flags`, `linker.args`, `cargo.unstable`, `cargo.target_json`, `linker.version_script`). Users get `Error: unknown key` for documented fields. Additionally, `ts backup`, `ts restore`, and `ts new -f` round-trip through serde serialization, which destroys user comments and formatting.\n\n### Three write strategies\n\n| Operation | Strategy | Why |\n|---|---|---|\n| load, hash, build | serde (`toml`) | Need typed `Spec` struct for build logic |\n| set, unset | `toml_edit` (`DocumentMut`) | Surgical edit preserving comments/formatting |\n| backup, restore, new -f | raw `fs::copy` | Exact byte-for-byte preservation |\n\n### Step 1: Add `toml_edit` dependency\n\n**File:** `Cargo.toml`\n\nAdd `toml_edit = \"0.22\"` to `[dependencies]`. Already a transitive dep via `toml 0.8`, so no new download.\n\n### Step 2: Create `src/ts_cmd/edit.rs` - toml_edit helper module\n\nNew module with:\n\n- **`FIELD_REGISTRY`** - static list of all valid field paths with their types:\n  ```\n  (\"panic\", Scalar)           (\"strip\", Scalar)\n  (\"cargo.profile\", Scalar)   (\"cargo.target_triple\", Scalar)\n  (\"cargo.target_json\", Scalar) (\"cargo.target_dir\", Scalar)\n  (\"cargo.unstable\", Array)   (\"rustc.opt_level\", Scalar)\n  (\"rustc.panic\", Scalar)     (\"rustc.lto\", Scalar)\n  (\"rustc.codegen_units\", Scalar) (\"rustc.build_std\", Array)\n  (\"rustc.flags\", Array)      (\"linker.args\", Array)\n  ```\n  (Skip `linker.version_script` for now - it's a nested table with `global` array + `local` string, more complex.)\n\n- **`validate_key(key) -> Result<FieldKind>`** - checks key against registry, returns `Scalar` or `Array`\n\n- **`set_field(doc, key, value) -> Result<()>`** - for scalars: parse key path -> ensure table exists -> set `doc[\"section\"][\"field\"] = value`. Value parsing: booleans as `toml_edit::value(true/false)`, integers as `toml_edit::value(i64)`, everything else as string. For arrays: parse `[a,b,c]` or `\"a\",\"b\"` syntax -> replace the array.\n\n- **`unset_field(doc, key) -> Result<()>`** - parse key path -> remove from table. If table becomes empty, remove the table too.\n\n- **`validate_value(key, value) -> Result<()>`** - reuse existing parse functions (`parse_panic_mode`, `parse_strip_mode`, etc.) for enum keys. Accept anything for string/array keys.\n\n### Step 3: Rewrite `src/ts_cmd/set.rs` to use toml_edit\n\nReplace serde round-trip with:\n1. Read file as raw string (or empty string if file doesn't exist)\n2. Parse into `DocumentMut`\n3. Validate key via `edit::validate_key()`\n4. Validate value via `edit::validate_value()` (for enum fields)\n5. Call `edit::set_field(doc, key, value)`\n6. Write `doc.to_string()` back to file\n\nRemove `apply_value()` and all parse helper functions from set.rs (move validation to edit.rs).\n\nUpdate tests to test the new toml_edit-based flow. Add tests for previously-missing keys (`rustc.build_std`, `linker.args`, `cargo.unstable`, etc.).\n\n### Step 4: Create `src/ts_cmd/unset.rs` - new command\n\nNew file:\n1. Resolve package dir and tspec path (same pattern as set.rs)\n2. Read file as raw string\n3. Parse into `DocumentMut`\n4. Validate key via `edit::validate_key()`\n5. Call `edit::unset_field(doc, key)`\n6. Write back\n\n### Step 5: Wire up `ts unset` in CLI\n\n**Files:** `src/cmd/ts.rs`, `src/ts_cmd/mod.rs`\n\n- Add `Unset` variant to `TsCommands` enum (same args as `Set` minus the value):\n  ```\n  Unset { key, package, tspec }\n  ```\n- Add `unset_value` to `ts_cmd/mod.rs` re-exports\n- Add match arm in `TsCmd::execute()`\n\n### Step 6: Convert `ts backup` to raw file copy\n\n**Files:** `src/ts_cmd/backup.rs`, `src/tspec.rs`\n\nCurrent flow: `load_spec` -> `save_spec_snapshot` (serde serialize) - destroys comments.\n\nNew flow:\n1. Find the spec path (same as now)\n2. `load_spec` **only for hashing** (to compute the hash for the backup filename)\n3. Compute next sequence number (same logic as `save_spec_snapshot`)\n4. `fs::copy(source_path, backup_path)` - exact byte-for-byte copy\n\nExtract the sequence-number logic from `save_spec_snapshot` into a helper `next_snapshot_seq(name, dir)` in `tspec.rs` so both old code and new backup can use it.\n\n### Step 7: Convert `ts restore` to raw file copy\n\n**File:** `src/ts_cmd/restore.rs`\n\nCurrent flow: `load_spec(backup)` -> `save_spec(target)` - destroys comments.\n\nNew flow:\n1. Find backup path (same as now)\n2. Parse base name (same as now)\n3. `fs::copy(backup_path, target_path)` - exact copy\n\n### Step 8: Convert `ts new -f` to raw file copy\n\n**File:** `src/ts_cmd/new.rs`\n\nCurrent flow: `load_spec(source)` -> `save_spec(target)` - destroys comments.\n\nNew flow when `--from` is provided:\n1. Resolve source spec path (same as now)\n2. `fs::copy(source_path, output_path)` - exact copy\n\nWhen `--from` is NOT provided, keep using serde `save_spec(&Spec::default(), ...)` since there's no source to copy.\n\n### Step 9 (nice-to-have): Array append/remove syntax in `ts set`\n\nExtend `ts set` to support:\n- `tspec ts set linker.args+=-Wl,--gc-sections` (append to array)\n- `tspec ts set linker.args-=-Wl,--gc-sections` (remove from array)\n- `tspec ts set linker.args=[\"-static\",\"-nostdlib\"]` (replace entire array, from step 3)\n\nThis builds on the `edit::set_field` infrastructure. Can be deferred if the basic set/unset is solid.\n\n### Step 10: Update todo.md\n\nMark completed items, remove resolved entries.\n\n### Files to modify\n\n| File | Change |\n|---|---|\n| `Cargo.toml` | Add `toml_edit` dep |\n| `src/ts_cmd/edit.rs` | **NEW** - toml_edit helpers |\n| `src/ts_cmd/set.rs` | Rewrite to use toml_edit |\n| `src/ts_cmd/unset.rs` | **NEW** - unset command |\n| `src/ts_cmd/mod.rs` | Add unset + edit modules |\n| `src/cmd/ts.rs` | Add Unset variant |\n| `src/ts_cmd/backup.rs` | Use fs::copy |\n| `src/ts_cmd/restore.rs` | Use fs::copy |\n| `src/ts_cmd/new.rs` | Use fs::copy for --from |\n| `src/tspec.rs` | Extract `next_snapshot_seq()` helper |\n| `notes/todo.md` | Update status |\n\n### Verification\n\n```bash\ntspec test -p tspec          # All existing + new tests pass\ntspec clippy                 # No warnings\ntspec fmt --check            # Formatted\n\n# Manual smoke tests:\ntspec ts set rustc.build_std='[\"core\",\"alloc\"]'    # Previously: \"unknown key\"\ntspec ts set linker.args='[\"-static\"]'             # Previously: \"unknown key\"\ntspec ts unset rustc.lto                           # New command\ntspec ts backup && diff original backup            # Byte-identical\ntspec ts restore -t name-001-hash                  # Preserves comments\n```\n"
}
```
